- **assess-merge-risk**: Assess a GitHub PR as a merge decision rather than a code review — how reversible it is (two-way door or one-way), what its blast radius is if it is wrong, how loudly and how quickly that would surface, and what would have to change to make it safe to merge. Use when deciding whether to merge or sign off, and when asked "can we undo this", "is this a one-way door", "what's the blast radius", "how risky is this to merge", or before shipping anything touching money, customer comms, migrations, public contracts, or shared infrastructure.
